Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a quantum_mechanics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 128. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 256/2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by quantum_mechanics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 128, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 256. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on quantum_mechanics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 144. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 365 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 144 + O(1/n^365). Summing over all modes and applying the quantum_mechanics structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^614(S, F) is trivial. QED.
